  function readQty(){
    if(qtyApplied)return qtyApplied;
    try{
      var active=d.querySelector('.v-openbar .v-chip[aria-pressed="true"]');
      if(active){var m=(active.textContent||"").match(/\d+/);if(m){var n=Number(m[0]);if(n>0)return n;}}
    }catch(e){}
    return 1;
  }

    }
